SuperHubDoc-breadcrumb3CiEhI-z7_no"><ol class="Breadcrumb-list3wDDSRTNEBCQ rm-Breadcrumb-list"><li class="Breadcrumb-itemhU0JQW_9l3fK rm-Breadcrumb-item"><span>Encryption</span></li></ol></div><h1>AES-256 Encryption</h1><div class="excerpt"></div></div></div></header><div class="grid-container-fluid" id="content-container"><section class="content-body grid-75 "><div class="rm-Markdown markdown-body rm-Markdown markdown-body ng-non-bindable" data-testid="RDMD" style="margin-left:31px"><style>/*! tailwindcss v4.1.17 | MIT License | https://tailwindcss.com */
@layer theme, base, components, utilities;
@layer utilities;
</style><p>Encrypt every S2S Payment Gateway request and decrypt every response using AES-256-CBC with your merchant-specific encryption key and IV.</p>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="encryption-model"></div><div class="heading-text">Encryption Model</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Encryption Model" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#encryption-model"></a></h2>
<p>The gateway uses symmetric encryption: the same AES-256 key and initialization vector (IV) are used by your server and the gateway to encrypt and decrypt payloads.</p>

<pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">Merchant Server                    S2S Payment Gateway
      │                                     │
      │ 1. Build JSON payload               │
      │ 2. Encrypt with AES-256-CBC         │
      │ 3. Send encryptedData over HTTPS    │
      ├────────────────────────────────────►│
      │                                     │ 4. Decrypt payload
      │                                     │ 5. Process request
      │                                     │ 6. Encrypt response
      │ 7. Receive encryptedData            │
      │◄────────────────────────────────────┤
      │ 8. Decrypt and parse JSON           │</code></pre>

<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="encrypted-request-format"></div><div class="heading-text">Encrypted Request Format</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Encrypted Request Format" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#encrypted-request-format"></a></h2>
<p>All JSON request bodies are wrapped as Base64-encoded ciphertext:</p>

<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="json">JSON</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">{
  &quot;encryptedData&quot;: &quot;&lt;base64-aes-256-cbc-ciphertext&gt;&quot;
}</code></pre></div></div>
<p>The plaintext before encryption is the normal API-specific JSON payload.</p>
<p>Example plaintext for Verify VPA:</p>

<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="json">JSON</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">{
  &quot;mid&quot;: &quot;B10001&quot;,
  &quot;vpa&quot;: &quot;8898327678@upi&quot;
}</code></pre></div></div>
<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="encrypted-response-format"></div><div class="heading-text">Encrypted Response Format</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Encrypted Response Format" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#encrypted-response-format"></a></h2>
<p>Successful and error responses use the same wrapper:</p>

<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="json">JSON</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">{
  &quot;encryptedData&quot;: &quot;&lt;base64-aes-256-cbc-ciphertext&gt;&quot;
}</code></pre></div></div>
<p>After decryption, the response is a regular JSON object:</p>

<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="json">JSON</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">{
  &quot;vpaData&quot;: {
    &quot;vpa&quot;: &quot;8898327678@upi&quot;,
    &quot;isVPAValid&quot;: 1,
    &quot;payerAccountName&quot;: &quot;&quot;
  },
  &quot;response&quot;: {
    &quot;status&quot;: &quot;SUCCESS&quot;,
    &quot;statusCode&quot;: &quot;200&quot;
  }
}</code></pre></div></div>
<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="algorithm-requirements"></div><div class="heading-text">Algorithm Requirements</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Algorithm Requirements" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#algorithm-requirements"></a></h2>

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<div class="rdmd-table"><div class="rdmd-table-inner"><table><thead><tr><th>Property</th><th>Value</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Algorithm</td><td>AES</td></tr><tr><td>Key size</td><td>256 bits</td></tr><tr><td>Mode</td><td>CBC</td></tr><tr><td>Padding</td><td>PKCS#5 / PKCS#7</td></tr><tr><td>Ciphertext encoding</td><td>Base64</td></tr><tr><td>Key encoding</td><td>Hexadecimal or raw bytes, as provided during onboarding</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>
<div class="readme-tailwind"><blockquote class="callout callout_warning" theme="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation"><span class="callout-icon callout-icon_fa fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation"></span><p>Use the exact key and IV format provided during onboarding. If your credentials are supplied as hex strings, decode them before creating the cipher.</p></blockquote></div>
<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="code-samples"></div><div class="heading-text">Code Samples</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Code Samples" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#code-samples"></a></h2>

<div class="readme-tailwind"><div class="TabGroup"><header><nav class="TabGroup-nav"><button class="TabGroup-tab_active">Java</button><button class="TabGroup-tab">Node.js</button><button class="TabGroup-tab">PHP</button><button class="TabGroup-tab">Python</button></nav></header><section><div class="TabContent"><div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="java">Java</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">import javax.crypto.Cipher;
import javax.crypto.spec.IvParameterSpec;
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Base64;

public final class Aes256Cbc {
private Aes256Cbc() {}

```
public static String encrypt(String plainText, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {
    Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding&quot;);
    cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, &quot;AES&quot;), new IvParameterSpec(iv));
    byte[] encrypted = cipher.doFinal(plainText.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(encrypted);
}

public static String decrypt(String encryptedBase64, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {
    Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding&quot;);
    cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, &quot;AES&quot;), new IvParameterSpec(iv));
    byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encryptedBase64);
    return new String(cipher.doFinal(decoded), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
}
```

}</code></pre></div></div></div></section></div></div> <br />

<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="security-best-practices"></div><div class="heading-text">Security Best Practices</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Security Best Practices" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#security-best-practices"></a></h2>
<ol>
<li>Store encryption keys in a managed secret store.</li>
<li>Rotate keys only through a coordinated gateway-supported process.</li>
<li>Never log plaintext request or response bodies in production.</li>
<li>Mask PAN, CVV, VPA, phone number, and email fields in logs.</li>
<li>Reject decrypted responses that cannot be parsed as valid JSON.</li>
<li>Enforce TLS 1.2 or higher for outbound requests.</li>
<li>Apply PCI-DSS controls when handling card data server-side.</li>
</ol>
<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="common-encryption-errors"></div><div class="heading-text">Common Encryption Errors</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Common Encryption Errors" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#common-encryption-errors"></a></h2>

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<div class="rdmd-table"><div class="rdmd-table-inner"><table><thead><tr><th>Error</th><th>Cause</th><th>Resolution</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">Invalid key length</code></td><td>Key was not decoded correctly</td><td>Confirm whether key is hex, Base64, or raw text</td></tr><tr><td><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">bad decrypt</code></td><td>Wrong key, IV, or padding</td><td>Validate environment credentials and AES mode</td></tr><tr><td>Empty decrypted payload</td><td>Response body was not Base64 decoded</td><td>Base64-decode before decrypting</td></tr><tr><td>JSON parse failure</td><td>Decrypted text is not valid JSON</td><td>Log sanitized ciphertext reference ID and contact support</td></tr><tr><td>Encrypt every S2S Payment Gateway request and decrypt every response using AES-256-CBC with your merchant-specific encryption key and IV.</td><td></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>

<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="security-best-practices-1"></div><div class="heading-text">Security Best Practices</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Security Best Practices" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#security-best-practices-1"></a></h2>
<ol>
<li>Store encryption keys in a managed secret store.</li>
<li>Rotate keys only through a coordinated gateway-supported process.</li>
<li>Never log plaintext request or response bodies in production.</li>
<li>Mask PAN, CVV, VPA, phone number, and email fields in logs.</li>
<li>Reject decrypted responses that cannot be parsed as valid JSON.</li>

</ol>
<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="common-encryption-errors-1"></div><div class="heading-text">Common Encryption Errors</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Common Encryption Errors" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#common-encryption-errors-1"></a></h2>

<div class="rdmd-table"><div class="rdmd-table-inner"><table><thead><tr><th>Error</th><th>Cause</th><th>Resolution</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">Invalid key length</code></td><td>Key was not decoded correctly</td><td>Confirm whether key is hex, Base64, or raw text</td></tr><tr><td><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">bad decrypt</code></td><td>Wrong key, IV, or padding</td><td>Validate environment credentials and AES mode</td></tr><tr><td>Empty decrypted payload</td><td>Response body was not Base64 decoded</td><td>Base64-decode before decrypting</td></tr><tr><td>JSON parse failure</td><td>Decrypted text is not valid JSON</td><td>Log sanitized ciphertext reference ID and contact support</td></tr><tr><td>Encrypt every S2S Payment Gateway request and decrypt every response using AES-256-CBC with your merchant-specific encryption key and IV.</td><td></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="encryption-model-2"></div><div class="heading-text">Encryption Model</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Encryption Model" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#encryption-model-2"></a></h2>
<p>The gateway uses symmetric encryption: the same AES-256 key and initialization vector (IV) are used by your server and the gateway to encrypt and decrypt payloads.</p>

<pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">Merchant Server                    S2S Payment Gateway
      │                                     │
      │ 1. Build JSON payload               │
      │ 2. Encrypt with AES-256-CBC         │
      │ 3. Send encryptedData over HTTPS    │
      ├────────────────────────────────────►│
      │                                     │ 4. Decrypt payload
      │                                     │ 5. Process request
      │                                     │ 6. Encrypt response
      │ 7. Receive encryptedData            │
      │◄────────────────────────────────────┤
      │ 8. Decrypt and parse JSON           │</code></pre>

<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="encrypted-request-format-2"></div><div class="heading-text">Encrypted Request Format</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Encrypted Request Format" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#encrypted-request-format-2"></a></h2>
<p>All JSON request bodies are wrapped as Base64-encoded ciphertext:</p>

<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="json">JSON</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">{
  &quot;encryptedData&quot;: &quot;&lt;base64-aes-256-cbc-ciphertext&gt;&quot;
}</code></pre></div></div>
<p>The plaintext before encryption is the normal API-specific JSON payload.</p>
<p>Example plaintext for Verify VPA:</p>

<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="json">JSON</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">{
  &quot;mid&quot;: &quot;B10001&quot;,
  &quot;vpa&quot;: &quot;8898327678@upi&quot;
}</code></pre></div></div>
<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="encrypted-response-format-2"></div><div class="heading-text">Encrypted Response Format</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Encrypted Response Format" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#encrypted-response-format-2"></a></h2>
<p>Successful and error responses use the same wrapper:</p>

<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="json">JSON</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">{
  &quot;encryptedData&quot;: &quot;&lt;base64-aes-256-cbc-ciphertext&gt;&quot;
}</code></pre></div></div>
<p>After decryption, the response is a regular JSON object:</p>

<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="json">JSON</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">{
  &quot;vpaData&quot;: {
    &quot;vpa&quot;: &quot;8898327678@upi&quot;,
    &quot;isVPAValid&quot;: 1,
    &quot;payerAccountName&quot;: &quot;&quot;
  },
  &quot;response&quot;: {
    &quot;status&quot;: &quot;SUCCESS&quot;,
    &quot;statusCode&quot;: &quot;200&quot;
  }
}</code></pre></div></div>
<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="algorithm-requirements-2"></div><div class="heading-text">Algorithm Requirements</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Algorithm Requirements" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#algorithm-requirements-2"></a></h2>

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<div class="rdmd-table"><div class="rdmd-table-inner"><table><thead><tr><th>Property</th><th>Value</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Algorithm</td><td>AES</td></tr><tr><td>Key size</td><td>256 bits</td></tr><tr><td>Mode</td><td>CBC</td></tr><tr><td>Padding</td><td>PKCS#5 / PKCS#7</td></tr><tr><td>Ciphertext encoding</td><td>Base64</td></tr><tr><td>Key encoding</td><td>Hexadecimal or raw bytes, as provided during onboarding</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>
<div class="readme-tailwind"><blockquote class="callout callout_warning" theme="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation"><span class="callout-icon callout-icon_fa fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation"></span><p>Use the exact key and IV format provided during onboarding. If your credentials are supplied as hex strings, decode them before creating the cipher.</p></blockquote></div>
<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="code-samples-2"></div><div class="heading-text">Code Samples</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Code Samples" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#code-samples-2"></a></h2>

<div class="readme-tailwind"><div class="TabGroup"><header><nav class="TabGroup-nav"><button class="TabGroup-tab_active">Java</button><button class="TabGroup-tab">Node.js</button><button class="TabGroup-tab">PHP</button><button class="TabGroup-tab">Python</button></nav></header><section><div class="TabContent"><div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="java">Java</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">import javax.crypto.Cipher;
import javax.crypto.spec.IvParameterSpec;
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Base64;

public final class Aes256Cbc {
private Aes256Cbc() {}

```
public static String encrypt(String plainText, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {
    Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding&quot;);
    cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, &quot;AES&quot;), new IvParameterSpec(iv));
    byte[] encrypted = cipher.doFinal(plainText.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(encrypted);
}

public static String decrypt(String encryptedBase64, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {
    Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding&quot;);
    cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, &quot;AES&quot;), new IvParameterSpec(iv));
    byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encryptedBase64);
    return new String(cipher.doFinal(decoded), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
}
```

}</code></pre></div></div></div></section></div></div> <br />

<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="security-best-practices-2"></div><div class="heading-text">Security Best Practices</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Security Best Practices" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#security-best-practices-2"></a></h2>
<ol>
<li>Store encryption keys in a managed secret store.</li>
<li>Rotate keys only through a coordinated gateway-supported process.</li>
<li>Never log plaintext request or response bodies in production.</li>
<li>Mask PAN, CVV, VPA, phone number, and email fields in logs.</li>
<li>Reject decrypted responses that cannot be parsed as valid JSON.</li>
<li>Enforce TLS 1.2 or higher for outbound requests.</li>
<li>Apply PCI-DSS controls when handling card data server-side.</li>
</ol>
<br/>

<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="code-samples-4"></div><div class="heading-text">Code Samples</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Code Samples" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#code-samples-4"></a></h2>

<div class="readme-tailwind"><div class="TabGroup"><header><nav class="TabGroup-nav"><button class="TabGroup-tab_active">Java</button><button class="TabGroup-tab">Node.js</button><button class="TabGroup-tab">PHP</button><button class="TabGroup-tab">Python</button></nav></header><section><div class="TabContent"><div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="java">Java</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">import javax.crypto.Cipher;
import javax.crypto.spec.IvParameterSpec;
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Base64;

public final class Aes256Cbc {
private Aes256Cbc() {}

```
public static String encrypt(String plainText, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {
    Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding&quot;);
    cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, &quot;AES&quot;), new IvParameterSpec(iv));
    byte[] encrypted = cipher.doFinal(plainText.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(encrypted);
}

public static String decrypt(String encryptedBase64, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {
    Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding&quot;);
    cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, &quot;AES&quot;), new IvParameterSpec(iv));
    byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encryptedBase64);
    return new String(cipher.doFinal(decoded), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
}
```

}</code></pre></div></div></div></section></div></div> <br />

<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="security-best-practices-4"></div><div class="heading-text">Security Best Practices</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Security Best Practices" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#security-best-practices-4"></a></h2>
<ol>
<li>Store encryption keys in a managed secret store.</li>
<li>Rotate keys only through a coordinated gateway-supported process.</li>
<li>Never log plaintext request or response bodies in production.</li>
<li>Mask PAN, CVV, VPA, phone number, and email fields in logs.</li>
<li>Reject decrypted responses that cannot be parsed as valid JSON.</li>
<li>Enforce TLS 1.2 or higher for outbound requests.</li>
<li>Apply PCI-DSS controls when handling card data server-side.</li>
</ol>
<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="common-encryption-errors-4"></div><div class="heading-text">Common Encryption Errors</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Common Encryption Errors" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#common-encryption-errors-4"></a></h2>

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<div class="rdmd-table"><div class="rdmd-table-inner"><table><thead><tr><th>Error</th><th>Cause</th><th>Resolution</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">Invalid key length</code></td><td>Key was not decoded correctly</td><td>Confirm whether key is hex, Base64, or raw text</td></tr><tr><td><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">bad decrypt</code></td><td>Wrong key, IV, or padding</td><td>Validate environment credentials and AES mode</td></tr><tr><td>Empty decrypted payload</td><td>Response body was not Base64 decoded</td><td>Base64-decode before decrypting</td></tr><tr><td>JSON parse failure</td><td>Decrypted text is not valid JSON</td><td>Log sanitized ciphertext reference ID and contact support</td></tr><tr><td>Encrypt every S2S Payment Gateway request and decrypt every response using AES-256-CBC with your merchant-specific encryption key and IV.</td><td></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="encryption-model-5"></div><div class="heading-text">Encryption Model</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Encryption Model" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#encryption-model-5"></a></h2>
<p>The gateway uses symmetric encryption: the same AES-256 key and initialization vector (IV) are used by your server and the gateway to encrypt and decrypt payloads.</p>

<pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">Merchant Server                    S2S Payment Gateway
      │                                     │
      │ 1. Build JSON payload               │
      │ 2. Encrypt with AES-256-CBC         │
      │ 3. Send encryptedData over HTTPS    │
      ├────────────────────────────────────►│
      │                                     │ 4. Decrypt payload
      │                                     │ 5. Process request
      │                                     │ 6. Encrypt response
      │ 7. Receive encryptedData            │
      │◄────────────────────────────────────┤
      │ 8. Decrypt and parse JSON           │</code></pre>

<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="encrypted-request-format-5"></div><div class="heading-text">Encrypted Request Format</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Encrypted Request Format" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#encrypted-request-format-5"></a></h2>
<p>All JSON request bodies are wrapped as Base64-encoded ciphertext:</p>

<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="json">JSON</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">{
  &quot;encryptedData&quot;: &quot;&lt;base64-aes-256-cbc-ciphertext&gt;&quot;
}</code></pre></div></div>
<p>The plaintext before encryption is the normal API-specific JSON payload.</p>
<p>Example plaintext for Verify VPA:</p>

<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="json">JSON</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">{
  &quot;mid&quot;: &quot;B10001&quot;,
  &quot;vpa&quot;: &quot;8898327678@upi&quot;
}</code></pre></div></div>
<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="encrypted-response-format-5"></div><div class="heading-text">Encrypted Response Format</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Encrypted Response Format" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#encrypted-response-format-5"></a></h2>
<p>Successful and error responses use the same wrapper:</p>

<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="json">JSON</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">{
  &quot;encryptedData&quot;: &quot;&lt;base64-aes-256-cbc-ciphertext&gt;&quot;
}</code></pre></div></div>
<p>After decryption, the response is a regular JSON object:</p>

<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="json">JSON</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">{
  &quot;vpaData&quot;: {
    &quot;vpa&quot;: &quot;8898327678@upi&quot;,
    &quot;isVPAValid&quot;: 1,
    &quot;payerAccountName&quot;: &quot;&quot;
  },
  &quot;response&quot;: {
    &quot;status&quot;: &quot;SUCCESS&quot;,
    &quot;statusCode&quot;: &quot;200&quot;
  }
}</code></pre></div></div>
<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="algorithm-requirements-5"></div><div class="heading-text">Algorithm Requirements</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Algorithm Requirements" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#algorithm-requirements-5"></a></h2>

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<br />

<div class="rdmd-table"><div class="rdmd-table-inner"><table><thead><tr><th>Property</th><th>Value</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Algorithm</td><td>AES</td></tr><tr><td>Key size</td><td>256 bits</td></tr><tr><td>Mode</td><td>CBC</td></tr><tr><td>Padding</td><td>PKCS#5 / PKCS#7</td></tr><tr><td>Ciphertext encoding</td><td>Base64</td></tr><tr><td>Key encoding</td><td>Hexadecimal or raw bytes, as provided during onboarding</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>
<div class="readme-tailwind"><blockquote class="callout callout_warning" theme="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation"><span class="callout-icon callout-icon_fa fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation"></span><p>Use the exact key and IV format provided during onboarding. If your credentials are supplied as hex strings, decode them before creating the cipher.</p></blockquote></div>
<br/>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="code-samples-5"></div><div class="heading-text">Code Samples</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Code Samples" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#code-samples-5"></a></h2>

<div class="readme-tailwind"><div class="TabGroup"><header><nav class="TabGroup-nav"><button class="TabGroup-tab_active">Java</button><button class="TabGroup-tab">Node.js</button><button class="TabGroup-tab">PHP</button><button class="TabGroup-tab">Python</button></nav></header><section><div class="TabContent"><div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="java">Java</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">import javax.crypto.Cipher;
import javax.crypto.spec.IvParameterSpec;
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Base64;

public final class Aes256Cbc {
private Aes256Cbc() {}

```
public static String encrypt(String plainText, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {
    Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding&quot;);
    cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, &quot;AES&quot;), new IvParameterSpec(iv));
    byte[] encrypted = cipher.doFinal(plainText.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(encrypted);
}

public static String decrypt(String encryptedBase64, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {
    Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding&quot;);
    cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, &quot;AES&quot;), new IvParameterSpec(iv));
    byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encryptedBase64);
    return new String(cipher.doFinal(decoded), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
}
```

}</code></pre></div></div></div></section></div></div> <br />

<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="security-best-practices-5"></div><div class="heading-text">Security Best Practices</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Security Best Practices" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#security-best-practices-5"></a></h2>
<ol>
<li>Store encryption keys in a managed secret store.</li>
<li>Rotate keys only through a coordinated gateway-supported process.</li>
<li>Never log plaintext request or response bodies in production.</li>
<li>Mask PAN, CVV, VPA, phone number, and email fields in logs.</li>
<li>Reject decrypted responses that cannot be parsed as valid JSON.</li>
<li>Enforce TLS 1.2 or higher for outbound requests.</li>
<li>Apply PCI-DSS controls when handling card data server-side.</li>
</ol>
<br/>

Encryption","type":"basic","updated_at":"2026-05-29T07:02:48.000Z","uri":"/branches/1.0_ai-edits-1780037645434/guides/encryption","appearance":{"icon":{"name":null,"type":null}},"category":{"uri":"/branches/1.0_ai-edits-1780037645434/categories/guides/Encryption"},"href":{"dash":"https://dash.readme.com/project/jodetx-payment-orchestration/v1.0_ai-edits-1780037645434/docs/encryption","hub":"https://jodetx-payment-orchestration.readme.io/docs/encryption","github_url":null},"links":{"project":"/projects/me"},"parent":{"uri":null},"privacy":{"view":"public"},"project":{"name":"Jodetx Payment Orchestration","subdomain":"jodetx-payment-orchestration","uri":"/projects/me"},"content":{"body":"Encrypt every S2S Payment Gateway request and decrypt every response using AES-256-CBC with your merchant-specific encryption key and IV.\n\n## Encryption Model\n\nThe gateway uses symmetric encryption: the same AES-256 key and initialization vector (IV) are used by your server and the gateway to encrypt and decrypt payloads.\n\n```\nMerchant Server                    S2S Payment Gateway\n      │                                     │\n      │ 1. Build JSON payload               │\n      │ 2. Encrypt with AES-256-CBC         │\n      │ 3. Send encryptedData over HTTPS    │\n      ├────────────────────────────────────►│\n      │                                     │ 4. Decrypt payload\n      │                                     │ 5. Process request\n      │                                     │ 6. Encrypt response\n      │ 7. Receive encryptedData            │\n      │◄────────────────────────────────────┤\n      │ 8. Decrypt and parse JSON           │\n```\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Encrypted Request Format\n\nAll JSON request bodies are wrapped as Base64-encoded ciphertext:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"encryptedData\": \"\u003cbase64-aes-256-cbc-ciphertext>\"\n}\n```\n\nThe plaintext before encryption is the normal API-specific JSON payload.\n\nExample plaintext for Verify VPA:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"mid\": \"B10001\",\n  \"vpa\": \"8898327678@upi\"\n}\n```\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Encrypted Response Format\n\nSuccessful and error responses use the same wrapper:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"encryptedData\": \"\u003cbase64-aes-256-cbc-ciphertext>\"\n}\n```\n\nAfter decryption, the response is a regular JSON object:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"vpaData\": {\n    \"vpa\": \"8898327678@upi\",\n    \"isVPAValid\": 1,\n    \"payerAccountName\": \"\"\n  },\n  \"response\": {\n    \"status\": \"SUCCESS\",\n    \"statusCode\": \"200\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Algorithm Requirements\n\n| Property | Value |\n| --- | --- |\n| Algorithm | AES |\n| Key size | 256 bits |\n| Mode | CBC |\n| Padding | PKCS#5 / PKCS#7 |\n| Ciphertext encoding | Base64 |\n| Key encoding | Hexadecimal or raw bytes, as provided during onboarding |\n\n\u003cCallout theme=\"warning\" icon=\"fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation\">\nUse the exact key and IV format provided during onboarding. If your credentials are supplied as hex strings, decode them before creating the cipher.\n\u003c/Callout>\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Code Samples\n\n\u003cTabs>\n\u003cTab title=\"Java\">\n```java\nimport javax.crypto.Cipher;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.IvParameterSpec;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.util.Base64;\n\npublic final class Aes256Cbc {\n    private Aes256Cbc() {}\n\n    public static String encrypt(String plainText, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {\n        Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(\"AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding\");\n        cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, \"AES\"), new IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] encrypted = cipher.doFinal(plainText.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n        return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(encrypted);\n    }\n\n    public static String decrypt(String encryptedBase64, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {\n        Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(\"AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding\");\n        cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, \"AES\"), new IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encryptedBase64);\n        return new String(cipher.doFinal(decoded), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n    }\n}\n```\n\u003c/Tab>\n\u003cTab title=\"Node.js\">\n```javascript\nconst crypto = require('crypto');\n\nfunction encryptJson(payload, keyHex, ivHex) {\n  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv(\n    'aes-256-cbc',\n    Buffer.from(keyHex, 'hex'),\n    Buffer.from(ivHex, 'hex')\n  );\n\n  let encrypted = cipher.update(JSON.stringify(payload), 'utf8', 'base64');\n  encrypted += cipher.final('base64');\n  return encrypted;\n}\n\nfunction decryptJson(encryptedBase64, keyHex, ivHex) {\n  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv(\n    'aes-256-cbc',\n    Buffer.from(keyHex, 'hex'),\n    Buffer.from(ivHex, 'hex')\n  );\n\n  let decrypted = decipher.update(encryptedBase64, 'base64', 'utf8');\n  decrypted += decipher.final('utf8');\n  return JSON.parse(decrypted);\n}\n\nmodule.exports = { encryptJson, decryptJson };\n```\n\u003c/Tab>\n\u003cTab title=\"PHP\">\n```php\n\u003c?php\nfunction encryptJson(array $payload, string $keyHex, string $ivHex): string {\n    $key = hex2bin($keyHex);\n    $iv  = hex2bin($ivHex);\n    $plaintext = json_encode($payload, JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES);\n\n    return openssl_encrypt(\n        $plaintext,\n        'AES-256-CBC',\n        $key,\n        OPENSSL_RAW_DATA,\n        $iv\n    );\n}\n\nfunction decryptJson(string $encryptedBase64, string $keyHex, string $ivHex): array {\n    $key = hex2bin($keyHex);\n    $iv  = hex2bin($ivHex);\n    $ciphertext = base64_decode($encryptedBase64);\n\n    $plaintext = openssl_decrypt(\n        $ciphertext,\n        'AES-256-CBC',\n        $key,\n        OPENSSL_RAW_DATA,\n        $iv\n    );\n\n    return json_decode($plaintext, true);\n}\n?>\n```\n\u003c/Tab>\n\u003cTab title=\"Python\">\n```python\nimport base64\nimport binascii\nimport json\nfrom Crypto.Cipher import AES\nfrom Crypto.Util.Padding import pad, unpad\n\ndef encrypt_json(payload: dict, key_hex: str, iv_hex: str) -> str:\n    key = binascii.unhexlify(key_hex)\n    iv = binascii.unhexlify(iv_hex)\n    cipher = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_CBC, iv)\n    plaintext = json.dumps(payload, separators=(\",\", \":\")).encode()\n    ciphertext = cipher.encrypt(pad(plaintext, AES.block_size))\n    return base64.b64encode(ciphertext).decode()\n\ndef decrypt_json(encrypted_base64: str, key_hex: str, iv_hex: str) -> dict:\n    key = binascii.unhexlify(key_hex)\n    iv = binascii.unhexlify(iv_hex)\n    cipher = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_CBC, iv)\n    plaintext = unpad(cipher.decrypt(base64.b64decode(encrypted_base64)), AES.block_size)\n    return json.loads(plaintext)\n```\n\u003c/Tab>\n\u003c/Tabs>\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Security Best Practices\n\n1. Store encryption keys in a managed secret store.\n2. Rotate keys only through a coordinated gateway-supported process.\n3. Never log plaintext request or response bodies in production.\n4. Mask PAN, CVV, VPA, phone number, and email fields in logs.\n5. Reject decrypted responses that cannot be parsed as valid JSON.\n6. Enforce TLS 1.2 or higher for outbound requests.\n7. Apply PCI-DSS controls when handling card data server-side.\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Common Encryption Errors\n\n| Error | Cause | Resolution |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `Invalid key length` | Key was not decoded correctly | Confirm whether key is hex, Base64, or raw text |\n| `bad decrypt` | Wrong key, IV, or padding | Validate environment credentials and AES mode |\n| Empty decrypted payload | Response body was not Base64 decoded | Base64-decode before decrypting |\n| JSON parse failure | Decrypted text is not valid JSON | Log sanitized ciphertext reference ID and contact support |\nEncrypt every S2S Payment Gateway request and decrypt every response using AES-256-CBC with your merchant-specific encryption key and IV.\n\n## Encryption Model\n\nThe gateway uses symmetric encryption: the same AES-256 key and initialization vector (IV) are used by your server and the gateway to encrypt and decrypt payloads.\n\n```\nMerchant Server                    S2S Payment Gateway\n      │                                     │\n      │ 1. Build JSON payload               │\n      │ 2. Encrypt with AES-256-CBC         │\n      │ 3. Send encryptedData over HTTPS    │\n      ├────────────────────────────────────►│\n      │                                     │ 4. Decrypt payload\n      │                                     │ 5. Process request\n      │                                     │ 6. Encrypt response\n      │ 7. Receive encryptedData            │\n      │◄────────────────────────────────────┤\n      │ 8. Decrypt and parse JSON           │\n```\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Encrypted Request Format\n\nAll JSON request bodies are wrapped as Base64-encoded ciphertext:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"encryptedData\": \"\u003cbase64-aes-256-cbc-ciphertext>\"\n}\n```\n\nThe plaintext before encryption is the normal API-specific JSON payload.\n\nExample plaintext for Verify VPA:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"mid\": \"B10001\",\n  \"vpa\": \"8898327678@upi\"\n}\n```\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Encrypted Response Format\n\nSuccessful and error responses use the same wrapper:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"encryptedData\": \"\u003cbase64-aes-256-cbc-ciphertext>\"\n}\n```\n\nAfter decryption, the response is a regular JSON object:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"vpaData\": {\n    \"vpa\": \"8898327678@upi\",\n    \"isVPAValid\": 1,\n    \"payerAccountName\": \"\"\n  },\n  \"response\": {\n    \"status\": \"SUCCESS\",\n    \"statusCode\": \"200\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Algorithm Requirements\n\n| Property | Value |\n| --- | --- |\n| Algorithm | AES |\n| Key size | 256 bits |\n| Mode | CBC |\n| Padding | PKCS#5 / PKCS#7 |\n| Ciphertext encoding | Base64 |\n| Key encoding | Hexadecimal or raw bytes, as provided during onboarding |\n\n\u003cCallout theme=\"warning\" icon=\"fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation\">\nUse the exact key and IV format provided during onboarding. If your credentials are supplied as hex strings, decode them before creating the cipher.\n\u003c/Callout>\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Code Samples\n\n\u003cTabs>\n\u003cTab title=\"Java\">\n```java\nimport javax.crypto.Cipher;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.IvParameterSpec;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.util.Base64;\n\npublic final class Aes256Cbc {\n    private Aes256Cbc() {}\n\n    public static String encrypt(String plainText, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {\n        Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(\"AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding\");\n        cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, \"AES\"), new IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] encrypted = cipher.doFinal(plainText.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n        return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(encrypted);\n    }\n\n    public static String decrypt(String encryptedBase64, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {\n        Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(\"AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding\");\n        cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, \"AES\"), new IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encryptedBase64);\n        return new String(cipher.doFinal(decoded), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n    }\n}\n```\n\u003c/Tab>\n\u003cTab title=\"Node.js\">\n```javascript\nconst crypto = require('crypto');\n\nfunction encryptJson(payload, keyHex, ivHex) {\n  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv(\n cipher.final('base64');\n  return encrypted;\n}\n\nfunction decryptJson(encryptedBase64, keyHex, ivHex) {\n  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv(\n    'aes-256-cbc',\n    Buffer.from(keyHex, 'hex'),\n    Buffer.from(ivHex, 'hex')\n  );\n\n  let decrypted = decipher.update(encryptedBase64, 'base64', 'utf8');\n  decrypted += decipher.final('utf8');\n  return Store encryption keys in a managed secret store.\n2. Rotate keys only through a coordinated gateway-supported process.\n3. Never log plaintext request or response bodies in production.\n4. Mask PAN, CVV, VPA, phone number, and email fields in logs.\n5. Reject decrypted responses that cannot be parsed as valid JSON.\n6. Enforce TLS 1.2 or higher for outbound requests.\n7. Apply PCI-DSS controls when handling card data server-side.\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Common Encryption Errors\n\n| Error | Cause | Resolution |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `Invalid key length` | Key was not decoded correctly | Confirm whether key is hex, Base64, or raw text |\n| `bad decrypt` | Wrong key, IV, or padding | Validate environment credentials and AES mode |\n| Empty decrypted payload | Response body was not Base64 decoded | Base64-decode before decrypting |\n| JSON parse failure | Decrypted text is not valid JSON | Log sanitized ciphertext reference ID and contact support |\nEncrypt every S2S Payment Gateway request and decrypt every response using AES-256-CBC with your merchant-specific encryption key and IV.\n\n## Encryption Model\n\nThe gateway uses symmetric encryption: the same AES-256 key and initialization vector (IV) are used by your server and the gateway to encrypt and decrypt payloads.\n\n```\nMerchant Server                    S2S Payment Gateway\n      │                                     │\n      │ 1. Build JSON payload               │\n      │ 2. Encrypt with AES-256-CBC         │\n      │ 3. Send encryptedData over HTTPS    │\n      ├────────────────────────────────────►│\n      │                                     │ 4. Decrypt payload\n      │                                     │ 5. Process request\n      │                                     │ 6. Encrypt response\n      │ 7. Receive encryptedData            │\n      │◄────────────────────────────────────┤\n      │ 8. Decrypt and parse JSON           │\n```\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Encrypted Request Format\n\nAll JSON request bodies are wrapped as Base64-encoded ciphertext:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"encryptedData\": \"\u003cbase64-aes-256-cbc-ciphertext>\"\n}\n```\n\nThe plaintext before encryption is the normal API-specific JSON payload.\n\nExample plaintext for Verify VPA:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"mid\": \"B10001\",\n  \"vpa\": \"8898327678@upi\"\n}\n```\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Encrypted Response Format\n\nSuccessful and error responses use the same wrapper:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"encryptedData\": \"\u003cbase64-aes-256-cbc-ciphertext>\"\n}\n```\n\nAfter decryption, the response is a regular JSON object:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"vpaData\": {\n    \"vpa\": \"8898327678@upi\",\n    \"isVPAValid\": 1,\n    \"payerAccountName\": \"\"\n  },\n  \"response\": {\n    \"status\": \"SUCCESS\",\n    \"statusCode\": \"200\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Algorithm Requirements\n\n| Property | Value |\n| --- | --- |\n| Algorithm | AES |\n| Key size | 256 bits |\n| Mode | CBC |\n| Padding | PKCS#5 / PKCS#7 |\n| Ciphertext encoding | Base64 |\n| Key encoding | Hexadecimal or raw bytes, as provided during onboarding |\n\n\u003cCallout theme=\"warning\" icon=\"fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation\">\nUse the exact key and IV format provided during onboarding. If your credentials are supplied as hex strings, decode them before creating the cipher.\n\u003c/Callout>\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Code Samples\n\n\u003cTabs>\n\u003cTab title=\"Java\">\n```java\nimport javax.crypto.Cipher;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.IvParameterSpec;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.util.Base64;\n\npublic final class Aes256Cbc {\n    private Aes256Cbc() {}\n\n    public static String encrypt(String plainText, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {\n        Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(\"AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding\");\n        cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, \"AES\"), new IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] encrypted = cipher.doFinal(plainText.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n        return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(encrypted);\n    }\n\n    public static String decrypt(String encryptedBase64, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {\n        Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(\"AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding\");\n        cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, \"AES\"), new IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encryptedBase64);\n        return new String(cipher.doFinal(decoded), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n    }\n}\n```\n\u003c/Tab>\n\u003cTab title=\"Node.js\">\n```javascript\nconst crypto = require('crypto');\n\nfunction encryptJson(payload, keyHex, ivHex) {\n  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv(\n    'aes-256-cbc',\n    Buffer.from(keyHex, 'hex'),\n    Buffer.from(ivHex, 'hex')\n  );\n\n  let encrypted = cipher.update(JSON.stringify(payload), 'utf8', 'base64');\n  encrypted += cipher.final('base64');\n  return encrypted;\n}\n\nfunction decryptJson(encryptedBase64, keyHex, ivHex) {\n  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv(\n    'aes-256-cbc',\n    Buffer.from(keyHex, 'hex'),\n    Buffer.from(ivHex, 'hex')\n  );\n\n  let decrypted = decipher.update(encryptedBase64, 'base64', 'utf8');\n  decrypted += decipher.final('utf8');\n  return JSON.parse(decrypted);\n}\n\nmodule.exports = { encryptJson, decryptJson };\n```\n\u003c/Tab>\n\u003cTab title=\"PHP\">\n```php\n\u003c?php\nfunction encryptJson(array $payload, string $keyHex, string $ivHex): string {\n    $key = hex2bin($keyHex);\n    $iv  = hex2bin($ivHex);\n    $plaintext = json_encode($payload, JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES);\n\n    return openssl_encrypt(\n        $plaintext,\n        'AES-256-CBC',\n        $key,\n        OPENSSL_RAW_DATA,\n        $iv\n    );\n}\n\nfunction decryptJson(string $encryptedBase64, string $keyHex, string $ivHex): array {\n    $key = hex2bin($keyHex);\n    $iv  = hex2bin($ivHex);\n    $ciphertext = base64_decode($encryptedBase64);\n\n    $plaintext = openssl_decrypt(\n        $ciphertext,\n        'AES-256-CBC',\n        $key,\n        OPENSSL_RAW_DATA,\n        $iv\n    );\n\n    return json_decode($plaintext, true);\n}\n?>\n```\n\u003c/Tab>\n\u003cTab title=\"Python\">\n```python\nimport base64\nimport binascii\nimport json\nfrom Crypto.Cipher import AES\nfrom Crypto.Util.Padding import pad, unpad\n\ndef encrypt_json(payload: dict, key_hex: str, iv_hex: str) -> str:\n    key = binascii.unhexlify(key_hex)\n    iv = binascii.unhexlify(iv_hex)\n    cipher = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_CBC, iv)\n    plaintext = json.dumps(payload, separators=(\",\", \":\")).encode()\n    ciphertext = cipher.encrypt(pad(plaintext, AES.block_size))\n    return base64.b64encode(ciphertext).decode()\n\ndef decrypt_json(encrypted_base64: str, key_hex: str, iv_hex: str) -> dict:\n    key = binascii.unhexlify(key_hex)\n    iv = binascii.unhexlify(iv_hex)\n    cipher = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_CBC, iv)\n    plaintext = unpad(cipher.decrypt(base64.b64decode(encrypted_base64)), AES.block_size)\n    return json.loads(plaintext)\n```\n\u003c/Tab>\n\u003c/Tabs>\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Security Best Practices\n\n1. Store encryption keys in a managed secret store.\n2. Rotate keys only through a coordinated gateway-supported process.\n3. Never log plaintext request or response bodies in production.\n4. Mask PAN, CVV, VPA, phone number, and email fields in logs.\n5. Reject decrypted responses that cannot be parsed as valid JSON.\n6. Enforce TLS 1.2 or higher for outbound requests.\n7. Apply PCI-DSS controls when handling card data server-side.\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Common Encryption Errors\n\n| Error | Cause | Resolution |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `Invalid key length` | Key was not decoded correctly | Confirm whether key is hex, Base64, or raw text |\n| `bad decrypt` | Wrong key, IV, or padding | Validate environment credentials and AES mode |\n| Empty decrypted payload | Response body was not Base64 decoded | Base64-decode before decrypting |\n| JSON parse failure | Decrypted text is not valid JSON | Log sanitized ciphertext reference ID and contact support |\nEncrypt every S2S Payment Gateway request and decrypt every response using AES-256-CBC with your merchant-specific encryption key and IV.\n\n## Encryption Model\n\nThe gateway uses symmetric encryption: the same AES-256 key and initialization vector (IV) are used by your server and the gateway to encrypt and decrypt payloads.\n\n```\nMerchant Server                    S2S Payment Gateway\n      │                                     │\n      │ 1. Build JSON payload               │\n      │ 2. Encrypt with AES-256-CBC         │\n      │ 3. Send encryptedData over HTTPS    │\n      ├────────────────────────────────────►│\n      │                                     │ 4. Decrypt payload\n      │                                     │ 5. Process request\n      │                                     │ 6. Encrypt response\n      │ 7. Receive encryptedData            │\n      │◄────────────────────────────────────┤\n      │ 8. Decrypt and parse JSON           │\n```\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Encrypted Request Format\n\nAll JSON request bodies are wrapped as Base64-encoded ciphertext:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"encryptedData\": \"\u003cbase64-aes-256-cbc-ciphertext>\"\n}\n```\n\nThe plaintext before encryption is the normal API-specific JSON payload.\n\nExample plaintext for Verify VPA:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"mid\": \"B10001\",\n  \"vpa\": \"8898327678@upi\"\n}\n```\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Encrypted Response Format\n\nSuccessful and error responses use the same wrapper:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"encryptedData\": \"\u003cbase64-aes-256-cbc-ciphertext>\"\n}\n```\n\nAfter decryption, the response is a regular JSON object:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"vpaData\": {\n    \"vpa\": \"8898327678@upi\",\n    \"isVPAValid\": 1,\n    \"payerAccountName\": \"\"\n  },\n  \"response\": {\n    \"status\": \"SUCCESS\",\n    \"statusCode\": \"200\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Algorithm Requirements\n\n| Property | Value |\n| --- | --- |\n| Algorithm | AES |\n| Key size | 256 bits |\n| Mode | CBC |\n| Padding | PKCS#5 / PKCS#7 |\n| Ciphertext encoding | Base64 |\n| Key encoding | Hexadecimal or raw bytes, as provided during onboarding |\n\n\u003cCallout theme=\"warning\" icon=\"fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation\">\nUse the exact key and IV format provided during onboarding. If your credentials are supplied as hex strings, decode them before creating the cipher.\n\u003c/Callout>\n\n\u003cbr />\n\n## Code Samples\n\n\u003cTabs>\n\u003cTab title=\"Java\">\n```java\nimport javax.crypto.Cipher;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.IvParameterSpec;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.util.Base64;\n\npublic final class Aes256Cbc {\n    private Aes256Cbc() {}\n\n    public static String encrypt(String plainText, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {\n        Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(\"AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding\");\n        cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, \"AES\"), new IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] encrypted = cipher.doFinal(plainText.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n        return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(encrypted);\n    }\n\n    public static String decrypt(String encryptedBase64, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {\n        Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(\"AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding\");\n        cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, \"AES\"), new IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encryptedBase64);\n        return new String(cipher.doFinal(decoded), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n    }\n}\n```\n\u003c/Tab>\n\u003cTab title=\"Node.js\">\n```javascript\nconst crypto = require('crypto');\n\nfunction encryptJson(payload, keyHex, ivHex) {\n  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv(\n 'hex'),\n    Buffer.from(ivHex, 'hex')\n  );\n\n  let decrypted = decipher.update(encryptedBase64, 'base64', 'utf8');\n  decrypted += decipher.final('utf8');\n  return JSON.parse(decrypted);\n}\n\nmodule.exports = { encryptJson, decryptJson };\n```\n\u003c/Tab>\n\u003cTab title=\"PHP\">\n```php\n\u003c?php\nfunction encryptJson(array $payload, string $keyHex, string json\nfrom Crypto.Cipher import AES\nfrom Crypto.Util.Padding import pad, unpad\n\ndef encrypt_json(payload: dict, key_hex: str, iv_hex: str) -> str:\n    key = binascii.unhexlify(key_hex)\n    iv = If your credentials are supplied as hex strings, decode them before creating the cipher.\u003c/p>\u003c/blockquote>\u003c/div>\n\u003cbr/>\n\u003ch2 class=\"heading heading-2 header-scroll\">\u003cdiv CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined\">\u003cdiv class=\"CodeTabs-toolbar\">\u003cbutton type=\"button\" value=\"java\">Java\u003c/button>\u003c/div>\u003cdiv class=\"CodeTabs-inner\">\u003cpre>\u003ccode class=\"rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined\" data-lang=\"\">import javax.crypto.Cipher;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.IvParameterSpec;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.util.Base64;\n\npublic final class Aes256Cbc {\n    private Aes256Cbc() {}\n\n    public static String encrypt(String plainText, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {\n        Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding&quot;);\n        cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, &quot;AES&quot;), new IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] encrypted = cipher.doFinal(plainText.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n        return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(encrypted);\n    }\n\n    public static String decrypt(String encryptedBase64, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {\n        Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding&quot;);\n        cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, &quot;AES&quot;), new IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encryptedBase64);\n        return new String(cipher.doFinal(decoded), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n    }\n}\u003c/code>\u003c/pre>\u003c/div>\u003c/div>\u003c/div>\u003c/section>\u003c/div>\u003c/div>\n\u003cbr/>\n\u003ch2 class=\"heading heading-2 header-scroll\">\u003cdiv class=\"heading-anchor anchor waypoint\" id=\"security-best-practices\">\u003c/div>\u003cdiv class=\"heading-text\">Security Best Practices\u003c/div>\u003ca aria-label=\"Skip link to Security Best Practices\" class=\"heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor\" href=\"#security-best-practices\">\u003c/a>\u003c/h2>\n\u003col>\n\u003cli>Store encryption keys in a managed secret store.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Rotate keys only through a coordinated gateway-supported process.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Never log plaintext request or response bodies in production.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Mask PAN, CVV, VPA, phone number, and email fields in logs.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Reject decrypted responses that cannot be parsed as valid JSON.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Enforce TLS 1.2 or higher for outbound requests.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Apply PCI-DSS controls when handling card data server-side.\u003c/li>\n\u003c/ol>\n\u003cbr/>\n\u003ch2 class=\"heading heading-2 header-scroll\">\u003cdiv class=\"heading-anchor anchor waypoint\" id=\"common-encryption-errors\">\u003c/div>\u003cdiv class=\"heading-text\">Common Encryption encryption key and IV.\u003c/td>\u003ctd>\u003c/td>\u003ctd>\u003c/td>\u003c/tr>\u003c/tbody>\u003c/table>\u003c/div>\u003c/div>\n\u003ch2 class=\"heading heading-2 header-scroll\">\u003cdiv class=\"heading-anchor anchor waypoint\" id=\"encryption-model-1\">\u003c/div>\u003cdiv class=\"heading-text\">Encryption Model\u003c/div>\u003ca aria-label=\"Skip link to Encryption Model\" class=\"heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor\" href=\"#encryption-model-1\">\u003c/a>\u003c/h2>\n\u003cp>The gateway uses symmetric encryption: the same AES-256 key and initialization vector (IV) are used by your server and the gateway to encrypt and decrypt payloads.\u003c/p>\n\u003cpre>\u003ccode class=\"rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined\" data-lang=\"\">Merchant Server                    S2S Payment Gateway\n      │                                     │\n      │ 1. Build JSON payload               │\n      │ 2. Encrypt with AES-256-CBC         │\n      │ 3. Send encryptedData over HTTPS    │\n      ├────────────────────────────────────►│\n      │                                     │ 4. Decrypt payload\n      │                                     │ 5. Process request\n      │                                     │ 6. Encrypt response\n      │ 7. Receive encryptedData            │\n      │◄────────────────────────────────────┤\n      │ 8. Decrypt and parse JSON           │\u003c/code>\u003c/pre>\n\u003cbr/>\n\u003ch2 class=\"heading heading-2 header-scroll\">\u003cdiv class=\"heading-anchor anchor waypoint\" id=\"encrypted-request-format-1\">\u003c/div>\u003cdiv class=\"heading-text\">Encrypted Request Format\u003c/div>\u003ca aria-label=\"Skip link to Encrypted Request Format\" IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] encrypted = cipher.doFinal(plainText.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n        return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(encrypted);\n    }\n\n    public static CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined\">\u003cdiv class=\"CodeTabs-toolbar\">\u003cbutton type=\"button\" value=\"java\">Java\u003c/button>\u003c/div>\u003cdiv class=\"CodeTabs-inner\">\u003cpre>\u003ccode class=\"rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined\" data-lang=\"\">import javax.crypto.Cipher;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.IvParameterSpec;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.util.Base64;\n\npublic final class Aes256Cbc {\n    private Aes256Cbc() {}\n\n    public static String encrypt(String plainText, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {\n        Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding&quot;);\n        cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, &quot;AES&quot;), new IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] encrypted = cipher.doFinal(plainText.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n        return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(encrypted);\n    }\n\n    public static String decrypt(String encryptedBase64, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {\n        Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding&quot;);\n        cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, &quot;AES&quot;), new IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encryptedBase64);\n        return new String(cipher.doFinal(decoded), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n    }\n}\u003c/code>\u003c/pre>\u003c/div>\u003c/div>\u003c/div>\u003c/section>\u003c/div>\u003c/div>\n\u003cbr/>\n\u003ch2 class=\"heading heading-2 header-scroll\">\u003cdiv class=\"heading-anchor anchor waypoint\" id=\"security-best-practices-2\">\u003c/div>\u003cdiv class=\"heading-text\">Security Best Practices\u003c/div>\u003ca aria-label=\"Skip link to Security Best Practices\" class=\"heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor\" href=\"#security-best-practices-2\">\u003c/a>\u003c/h2>\n\u003col>\n\u003cli>Store encryption keys in a managed secret store.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Rotate keys only through a coordinated gateway-supported process.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Never log plaintext request or response bodies in production.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Mask PAN, CVV, VPA, phone number, and email fields in logs.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Reject decrypted responses that cannot be parsed as valid JSON.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Enforce TLS 1.2 or higher for outbound requests.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Apply PCI-DSS controls when handling card data server-side.\u003c/li>\n\u003c/ol>\n\u003cbr/>\n\u003ch2 class=\"heading heading-2 header-scroll\">\u003cdiv class=\"heading-anchor anchor waypoint\" id=\"common-encryption-errors-2\">\u003c/div>\u003cdiv class=\"heading-text\">Common Encryption text\u003c/td>\u003c/tr>\u003ctr>\u003ctd>\u003ccode class=\"rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined\" data-lang=\"\">bad decrypt\u003c/code>\u003c/td>\u003ctd>Wrong key, IV, or ciphertext reference ID and contact support\u003c/td>\u003c/tr>\u003ctr>\u003ctd>Encrypt every S2S Payment Gateway request and decrypt every response using AES-256-CBC with your merchant-specific encryption key and IV.\u003c/td>\u003ctd>\u003c/td>\u003ctd>\u003c/td>\u003c/tr>\u003c/tbody>\u003c/table>\u003c/div>\u003c/div>\n\u003ch2 class=\"heading heading-2 header-scroll\">\u003cdiv class=\"heading-anchor anchor waypoint\" id=\"encryption-model-3\">\u003c/div>\u003cdiv class=\"heading-text\">Encryption Model\u003c/div>\u003ca aria-label=\"Skip link to Encryption Model\" class=\"heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor\" href=\"#encryption-model-3\">\u003c/a>\u003c/h2>\n\u003cp>The gateway uses symmetric encryption: the same AES-256 key and initialization vector (IV) are used by your server and the gateway to encrypt and decrypt payloads.\u003c/p>\n\u003cpre>\u003ccode class=\"rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined\" data-lang=\"\">Merchant Server                    S2S Payment Gateway\n      │                                     │\n      │ 1. Build JSON payload               │\n      │ 2. Encrypt with AES-256-CBC         │\n      │ 3. Send encryptedData over HTTPS    │\n      ├────────────────────────────────────►│\n      │                                     │ 4. Decrypt payload\n      │                                     │ 5. Process request\n      │                                     │ 6. Encrypt response\n      │ 7. Receive encryptedData            │\n      │◄────────────────────────────────────┤\n      │ 8. Decrypt and parse JSON           │\u003c/code>\u003c/pre>\n\u003cbr/>\n\u003ch2 class=\"heading heading-2 header-scroll\">\u003cdiv class=\"heading-anchor anchor waypoint\" id=\"encrypted-request-format-3\">\u003c/div>\u003cdiv class=\"heading-text\">Encrypted Request Format\u003c/div>\u003ca aria-label=\"Skip link to Encrypted Request Format\" class=\"rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined\" data-lang=\"\">import javax.crypto.Cipher;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.IvParameterSpec;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.util.Base64;\n\npublic final class Aes256Cbc {\n    private Aes256Cbc() {}\n\n    public static String encrypt(String plainText, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {\n        Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding&quot;);\n        cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, &quot;AES&quot;), new IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] encrypted = cipher.doFinal(plainText.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n        return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(encrypted);\n    }\n\n    public static String decrypt(String encryptedBase64, byte[] key, byte[] iv) throws Exception {\n        Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding&quot;);\n        cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, new SecretKeySpec(key, &quot;AES&quot;), new IvParameterSpec(iv));\n        byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encryptedBase64);\n        return new String(cipher.doFinal(decoded), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n    }\n}\u003c/code>\u003c/pre>\u003c/div>\u003c/div>\u003c/div>\u003c/section>\u003c/div>\u003c/div>\n\u003cbr/>\n\u003ch2 class=\"heading aria-label=\"Skip link to Security Best Practices\" class=\"heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor\" href=\"#security-best-practices-4\">\u003c/a>\u003c/h2>\n\u003col>\n\u003cli>Store encryption keys in a managed secret store.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Rotate keys only through a coordinated gateway-supported process.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Never log plaintext request or response bodies in production.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Mask PAN, CVV, VPA, phone number, and email fields in logs.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Reject decrypted responses that cannot be parsed as valid JSON.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Enforce TLS 1.2 or higher for outbound requests.\u003c/li>\n\u003cli>Apply PCI-DSS controls when handling card data server-side.\u003c/li>\n\u003c/ol>\n\u003cbr/>\n\u003ch2 class=\"heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor\" href=\"#encryption-model-5\">\u003c/a>\u003c/h2>\n\u003cp>The gateway uses symmetric encryption: the same AES-256 key and initialization vector (IV) are used by your server and the gateway to encrypt and decrypt payloads.\u003c/p>\n\u003cpre>\u003ccode class=\"rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined\" data-lang=\"\">Merchant Server                    S2S Payment Gateway\n      │                                     │\n      │ 1. Build JSON payload               │\n      │ 2. Encrypt with AES-256-CBC         │\n      │ 3. Send encryptedData over HTTPS    │\n      ├────────────────────────────────────►│\n      │                                     │ 4. Decrypt payload\n      │                                     │ 5. Process request\n      │                                     │ 6. Encrypt response\n      │ 7. Receive encryptedData            │\n      │◄────────────────────────────────────┤\n      │ 8. Decrypt and parse JSON           │\u003c/code>\u003c/pre>\n\u003cbr/>\n\u003ch2 class=\"heading heading-2 header-scroll\">\u003cdiv class=\"heading-anchor anchor waypoint\"
